Implement smooth_obj_grad(x, n, beta, penalty_mu=0?) returns value and grad.
- c = np.correlate(x,1-x,full)
- maxc = c.max()
- scaled = beta*(c-maxc)
- exps = np.exp(scaled) (scaled ≤0, safe)
- s = exps.sum()
- w = exps / s
- val = dx * (maxc + np.log(s)/beta)

```
def smoother_full(x, beta, mu, target):
    x = np.clip(x,0,1) # to guard
    c = np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
    maxc = c.max()
    svals = np.exp(beta*(c-maxc))
    ssum = svals.sum()
    w = svals/sum
    val = (2.0/n) * (maxc + math.log(ssum)/beta)
    grad = smooth_grad_c(x,w)
    val += 0.5*mu*(x.sum()-target)**2
    grad += mu*(x.sum()-target)*np.ones(n)
    return val, grad
```
Need n from len(x). `np.exp` safe. If beta large, maxc maybe. Use `beta` up to 1e6. Scaled ≤0, log sum could large? beta*(c-maxc) very negative for inactive; exp underflows fine. If c all equal, ssum=length. log(s)/beta small. val ≈ maxc+avg slack. Good.

L-BFGS run helper:
```
def run_lbfgs_full(x, beta_grid, mu, max_iter, deadline):
  best=x.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(x)
  for beta in beta_grid:
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    try:
      res = minimize(_full_f, best, args=(beta,mu,len(best)/2), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=_full_g, options={'maxiter':max_iter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-7})
    except: pass
    cand = project_sum(np.clip(res.x,0,1), len(res.x))
    cand = np.clip(cand,0,1)
    c=exact_c5(cand)
    if c<best_c: best=cand; best_c=c
    # also try best from res.x not projected? project and evaluate
  return best,best_c
```

```
def cd_polish(h,n,passes,deadline, rng=None, lam=0.0, project_every=1):
  h=h.copy(); c=corr; cur=compute
  order maybe random
  for pass in range(passes):
    if time... break
    if rng: order=permutation else arange
    for p in order:
      h0 = h[p]
      if h0 < eps or >1-eps: continue
      x=solve_coord_fast(c,h,p,n,lam)
      if abs(x-h0)>... update
      c[n-1] -= delta*delta; h[p]=x
    err = h.sum()-n/2
    if abs(err)>1e-6:
       h = project_sum(h,n)
       c=corr
    # optional adjust lam based on err if lam not fixed
    if adaptive_lam: lam = -0.05*err clamp [-3,3]
    cur=compute c5 maybe each pass? expensive but O(n^2) cheap? compute_c5 uses correlate O(n^2) C, pass O(n^2) Python; compute negligible.
```

```
def weighted_grad_c(h,w):
    n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n)
    c0=w[n-1]
    if c0: g -= (2*h-1)*c0? Actually g += c0*(1-2h)
    for e in range(1,n):
       wp=w[n-1+e]; wn=w[n-1-e]
       if wp==0.0 and wn==0.0: continue
       if wp:
          g[:n-e] += wp*(1-h[e:])
          g[e:] -= wp*h[:n-e]
       if wn:
          g[e:] += wn*(1-h[:n-e])
          g[:n-e] -= wn*h[e:]
    return g
```

Fourier anti:
```
def random_anti_init(m, K, rng):
  t=np.arange(m)
  a=np.full(m,0.5)
  for k in range(1,K+1):
      amp=0.25/np.sqrt(k)
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      a += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t/m + phase)
  return np.clip(a,0,1)
```

```
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if not ordered: order = rng? But no rng parameter? pass None for fixed; if we want random, use a simple LCG?           ... update c,h
       err = h.sum()-n/2

           h = project_sum(h, n)
           c = corr
       cur = c5_from_c(c)
       if cur < best_c:

           # maybe revert? If projection worsened, but h still local? Reverting each time may converge to feasible best.
           # We can not revert to allow exploration, but track best.
```

```
def solve_coord_fast(c,h,p,n,lam=0.0,iters=30,tol=1e-13):
    h0=h[p]
    if h0<=tol or h0>=1-tol: return h0
    m0=n-1-p
    cb=c[m0:m0+n]
    a=np.empty(n)
    np.subtract(1.0,h,out=a)
    K = p if p < n-p else n-1-p? Actually K = min(p,n-1-p)
    st=p-K
    a[st:p+1] -= h[p:p+K+1][::-1]
    if lam: a += lam
    pos_idx = np.where(a > 1e-14)[0]
    neg_idx = np.where(a < -1e-14)[0]
    zero_any = idx? Could compute zero_count = n - len(pos)-len(neg); if zero_count: zero_max=...
    # But if diagonal included in zero, still handle quadratic; zero_max includes diag, okay.
    has_diag=True
    ad=a[p]; cd=cb[p]
    unaff = -1e300
    if m0>0: unaff=float(np.max(c[:m0]))
    end=m0+n
    if end < 2*n-1: unaff=max(unaff,float(np.max(c[end:])))
    cur_max = float(np.max(cb))
    if unaff>cur_max: cur_max=unaff
    if lam: cur_max += abs(lam)?? For L hi, max of affected with λ maybe cur_max + abs(lam); unaff line also. Use L_hi = cur_max + abs(lam) + 1.0
```

Bisection:
```
for _ in range(iters):
  L = 0.5*(L_lo+L_hi)
  lo=0.0; hi=1.0
  if zero_max > L+1e-12: feasible=False
  else:
    if len(pos_idx):
       hi = min(hi, float(np.min((L-cb[pos_idx])/a[pos_idx])))
    if len(neg_idx):
       lo = max(lo, float(np.max((L-cb[neg_idx])/a[neg_idx])))
    if unaff > -1e299:
       if lam > 1e-14:
          hi = min(hi, (L - (unaff - lam*h0))/lam)
       elif lam < -1e-14:
          lo = max(lo, (L - (unaff - lam*h0))/lam)
       elif unaff > L+1e-12: feasible=False
    # quadratic
    D = ad*ad + 4.0*(cd - L)
    if D >= 0:
       r1=(ad - sqrt(D))/2; r2=(ad + sqrt(D))/2
       x1=h0+r1; x2=h0+r2
       if not _quad_interval(lo,hi,h0,x1,x2): feasible=False
       else update lo/hi
  if feasible: L_hi=L else L_lo=L
```
Need `_quad_intersect` top-level returns (lo,hi,feasible). But to avoid function call inside bisection? Could inline. But code length. Top-level helper okay.

`_quad_intersect(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2)`: x1<x2. We can implement:
```
def quad_feasible_interval(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2):
    if hi <= x1 + 1e-12:
        return lo, hi, True
    if lo >= x2 - 1e-12:
        return lo, hi, True
    if lo < x1 and hi > x2:
        if abs(lo - x1) <= abs(hi - x2):
            hi = min(hi, x1)
        else:
            lo = max(lo, x2)
    else:
        if lo < x1:
            hi = min(hi, x1)
        if hi > x2:
            lo = max(lo, x2)
    if lo > hi + 1e-12:
        return 0,0,False
    return lo,hi,True
```

Need after pass project: `project_sum` Euclidean. Implement:
```
def project_sum(h,n):
    target = 0.5*n
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<1e-13: return h
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       p=np.clip(h+mid,0,1)
       ps=p.sum()
       if ps > target: hi=mid
       else: lo=mid
    p=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    # correct tiny error
    err=target-p.sum()
    if abs(err)>0 and not (np.all(p==0) or np.all(p==1)):
       # add err to interior values? simple if interior sum >0
       interior = (p>1e-12)&(p<1-1e-12)
       if np.any(interior):
           p[interior] += err / interior.sum()
           p=np.clip(p,0,1)
    return p
```
But bisection on mid with h already clipped: If target is within possible sum, works. Need if h outside [0,1], clip first changes objective; but okay. Sum after bisection maybe 1e-12. Correction by adding err to interior may break sum slightly if clipping; do iterative? Exact enough.
Could use Euclidean projection of unclipped h? If h within [0,1] mostly, clipping not needed except tiny. Good.

For anti h generated from a clip, sum exact? If a clipped within [0,1], sum h = ∑a + ∑(1-a)=m exactly? Floating maybe m + small. We can call project_sum on full h? But anti property may break. For anti, if a clipped, sum exact in exact arithmetic; floating sum of a and 1-a may not exactly m due round? It will be m + (sum a - sum a) maybe cancellation but 1-a computed; sum may be m within 1e-13. Fine.


Pseudo:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# constants eps

def _c5_from_corr(c,n): return float(np.max(c)*2.0/n)
def compute_c5(h): return _c5_from_corr(np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'), len(h))
def project_sum(h,n): ...
def anti_h(a):
   m=len(a); h=np.empty(2*m); h[:m]=a; h[m:]=1-a; return h
def anti_from_h(h):
   n=len(h); if n%2: return anti_h(anti_from_h(h[:-1])? )
   m=n//2; a=0.5*(h[:m] + 1.0 - h[m:]); return anti_h(np.clip(a,0,1))
def random_anti_init(m,K,rng): ...
def random_full_init(n,K,rng): ...

def weighted_grad_c(h,w): ...
```

```
def choose_quad_interval(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2):
    # x1 < x2 forbidden open (x1,x2); allowed closed outside.
    # clip lo/hi to [0,1]
    if lo < 0: lo=0.0
    if hi > 1: hi=1.0
    best_lo = -1.0
    best_len = -1.0
    # left allowed [lo, min(hi,x1)] if min(hi,x1) >= lo - tol
    end1 = min(hi, x1)
    if end1 >= lo - 1e-12:
        mid = 0.5*(lo+end1)
        # prefer segment containing h0? h0 is original, but h0 may be inside forbidden? It cannot? At x0, diagonal equals cd; for L* near cd? If L*<cd, x0 infeasible. h0 may be inside forbidden. We can prefer midpoint close to h0.
        best_lo=lo; best_hi=end1; best_dist=abs(mid-h0)
    else:
        best_dist=1e100
    end2 = max(lo, x2)
    if end2 <= hi + 1e-12:
        mid = 0.5*(end2+hi)
        dist=abs(mid-h0)
        if dist < best_dist - 1e-12:
            best_lo=end2; best_hi=hi; best_dist=dist
    if best_dist == 1e100:
        return None
    if best_lo > best_hi + 1e-12:
        # single point
        val = 0.5*(best_lo+best_hi)
        return val
    return 0.5*(best_lo+best_hi)
```

If end1>=lo. If x1 < lo, end1=x1<lo invalid. So condition `if lo <= x1 + 1e-12 and hi >= lo:`. For right: valid if hi >= x2.
Implement simpler:
```
def choose_quad_interval(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2):
   cands=[]
   if lo <= x1 + 1e-12:
      e = min(hi, x1)
      if e >= lo - 1e-12: cands.append((lo,e))
   if hi >= x2 - 1e-12:
      s = max(lo, x2)
      if s <= hi + 1e-12: cands.append((s,hi))
   if not cands: return None
   # choose based on distance of midpoint or closer to h0
   if len(cands)==1: lo1,hi1=cands[0]
   else:
      # if cands[0] midpoint closer
      m0=0.5*(cands[0][0]+cands[0][1]); m1=0.5*(cands[1][0]+cands[1][1])
      if abs(m0-h0) <= abs(m1-h0): lo1,hi1=cands[0] else cands[1]
   x=0.5*(lo1+hi1)
   return float(np.clip(x,0,1))
```

```
def cd_polish(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng=None,lam=0.0):
    h = np.array(h,float).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    cur = _c5_from_corr(c,n)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = cur
    base = np.arange(n)
    for it in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       if rng is not None:
          order = rng.permutation(n)
          # maybe make list? no
       else:
          shift = (it * 73) % n
          order = np.concatenate((base[shift:], base[:shift]))
       changed=0
       for p in order:
          if (p & 31)==31 and time.time()>deadline: break
          h0=h[p]
          if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1-1e-13: continue
          x=solve_coord_fast(c,h,p,n,lam)
          if x is None or abs(x-h0) <= 1e-13: continue
          m0=n-1-p; delta=x-h0
          c[m0:m0+n] += delta * a? Wait a local in solver! We need a for update. solve_coord_fast currently returns x only; a discarded. We need update c using a. Need adjust `solve_coord_fast` accordingly. Let's write:
```
```
def solve_coord_fast(c,h,p,n,lam=0.0,iters=30,tol=1e-13):
    h0=h[p]
    if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1-1e-14: return h0, None
    R = p if p >= n-1-p else n-1-p
    L = 2*R+1
    m0 = n-1-R
    d = np.arange(-R, R+1)  # dtype int
    a = np.zeros(L)
    q1 = p - d
    m1 = (q1 >= 0) & (q1 < n)
    if np.any(m1): a[m1] += 1.0 - h[q1[m1]]
    q2 = p + d
    m2 = (q2 >= 0) & (q2 < n)
    if np.any(m2): a[m2] -= h[q2[m2]]
    if lam != 0.0:
       av = a + lam
    else: av = a
    cb = c[m0:m0+L]
    diag_idx = R
    cd = cb[diag_idx]
    ad = av[diag_idx]
    unaff = -1e300
    if m0 > 0: unaff = float(np.max(c[:m0]))
    m_end = m0+L
    total_lags = 2*n-1
    if m_end < total_lags: unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[m_end:])))
    cur_max = float(np.max(cb))
    if unaff > cur_max: cur_max = unaff
    pos_idx = np.where(av > 1e-14)[0]
    neg_idx = np.where(av < -1e-14)[0]
    zero_mask = ~( (av > 1e-14) | (av < -1e-14) )
    zero_max = float(np.max(cb[zero_mask])) if np.any(zero_mask) else -1e300
    # Precompute arrays
    cb_pos = cb[pos_idx]; a_pos=av[pos_idx]
    cb_neg=cb[neg_idx]; a_neg=av[neg_idx]
    L_hi = cur_max
    if lam == 0.0:
       L_lo = 0.0
    else:
       L_lo = -abs(lam) - 1.0
    if L_hi < L_lo: L_hi = L_lo
    # bisection
    for _ in range(iters):
       L=0.5*(L_lo+L_hi)
       lo=0.0; hi=1.0; feasible=True
       if zero_max > L + 1e-12: feasible=False
       if feasible:
          if pos_idx.size:
             ub = h0 + float(np.min((L - cb_pos)/a_pos))
             if ub < hi: hi=ub
          if neg_idx.size:
             lb = h0 + float(np.max((L - cb_neg)/a_neg))
             if lb > lo: lo=lb
          if unaff > -1e299:
             if lam > 1e-14:
                ub = h0 + (L - unaff)/lam
                if ub < hi: hi=ub
             elif lam < -1e-14:
                lb = h0 + (L - unaff)/lam
                if lb > lo: lo=lb
             elif unaff > L + 1e-12:
                feasible=False
       if feasible and lo <= hi + 1e-12:
          D = ad*ad + 4.0*(cd - L)
          if D >= -1e-12:
             if D < 0: D=0.0
             sq = math.sqrt(D)
             x1 = h0 + 0.5*(ad - sq)
             x2 = h0 + 0.5*(ad + sq)
             xq = choose_quad_interval(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2)
             if xq is None: feasible=False
       if feasible: L_hi=L else L_lo=L
    # final choose
    L = L_hi
    lo=0.0; hi=1.0
    if zero_max > L + 1e-11:
        return h0, None
    if pos_idx.size: hi = min(hi, h0 + float(np.min((L - cb_pos)/a_pos)))
    if neg_idx.size: lo = max(lo, h0 + float(np.max((L - cb_neg)/a_neg)))
    if unaff > -1e299:
       if lam >1e-14: hi=min(hi, h0+(L-unaff)/lam)
       elif lam < -1e-14: lo=max(lo, h0+(L-unaff)/lam)
       elif unaff > L +1e-11: return h0,None
    if lo > hi + 1e-10:
       # try clip
       if lo < 0: lo=0
       if hi > 1: hi=1
       if lo > hi + 1e-8: return h0,None
    D=ad*ad+4.0*(cd-L)
    if D >= -1e-12:
       if D<0: D=0
       x1=h0+0.5*(ad-math.sqrt(D)); x2=h0+0.5*(ad+math.sqrt(D))
       x = choose_quad_interval(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2)
    else:
       x = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    if x is None: return h0,None
    x=float(np.clip(x,0,1))
    if abs(x-h0) <= 1e-13: return h0,None
    return x, a
```
Need note: If `lam` nonzero, `a` returned base. Good.

In bisection, if `L_hi = cur_max` and `L_lo` low, but at L=cur_max feasible. Bisection will narrow to minimal feasible L. If all c zero and lam=0, L_hi=0; L_lo=0; loop still 30 but L constant? If L_hi-L_lo small break? Add:
```
if L_hi - L_lo < 1e-13: break
```
inside.

`choose_quad_interval`: Need top-level, no lambda. We'll implement returning float or None.

Potential issue: For diagonal, `ad` includes lam. If lam nonzero and L is penalized, the quadratic inequality is c_diag + ad δ - δ² ≤ L. Correct. But final x may minimize penalized; actual max c maybe lower/higher. Use lam small.

```
x,a = solve_coord_fast(c,h,p,n,lam)
if a is None: continue
m0 = n-1 - max(p,n-1-p)  # need R again
R = max(...)
c[m0:m0+2*R+1] += delta * a
c[n-1] -= delta*delta
h[p]=x
```

This CD per coordinate allocates d, q arrays. Bisection uses `math.sqrt` scalar. Good.

Now, with correct orientation, L-BFGS gradient? Weighted gradient formula should match actual c orientation. We derived earlier for c_d=∑h_i(1-h_{i+d}) (opposite). Need update gradient to match actual NumPy c_s =∑ h_{i+d}(1-h_i) = ∑_j h_j(1-h_{j-d}). This is mirror of previous. Since objective max over all lags, gradient weights w array from c. We need compute derivative wrt h_p for each lag m (d = m-center). For c_d =∑ h_j(1-h_{j-d}):
- p as h factor j=p: derivative 1 - h[p-d] valid if 0≤p-d<n.
- p as 1-h factor i=p: derivative -h[p+d] valid if 0≤p+d<n.
Thus grad[p] contributions over d:
For each d, if p-d valid: grad[p] += w_d (1 - h[p-d])
if p+d valid: grad[p] -= w_d h[p+d]
This is symmetric to previous but with d signs swapped? Let's derive loop by e positive/negative.
We can use pair d=+e and d=-e:
For d=+e (lag index center+e): valid h factor if p-e≥0 => p≥e: grad[p] += wp (1-h[p-e]); second if p+e<n: grad[p] -= wp h[p+e].
Vector:
- p=e..n-1 add wp*(1-h[p-e]) => grad[e:] += wp*(1-h[:-e])? h[p-e] for p=e..n-1 is h[0..n-1-e] => grad[e:] += wp*(1-h[:-e])
- p=0..n-1-e subtract wp*h[p+e] => grad[:n-e] -= wp*h[e:]
For d=-e (lag center-e): first if p-(-e)=p+e valid => p≤n-1-e: grad[:n-e] += wn*(1-h[p+e]) = wn*(1-h[e:]); second if p+(-e)=p-e valid => p≥e: grad[e:] -= wn*h[p-e] = wn*h[:-e].
Thus:
```
g += w0*(1-2h)
for e in 1..n-1:
 wp = w[n-1+e]
 wn = w[n-1-e]
 if wp:
    g[e:] += wp*(1-h[:-e])
    g[:n-e] -= wp*h[e:]
 if wn:
    g[:n-e] += wn*(1-h[e:])
    g[e:] -= wn*h[:-e]
```
Compare previous gradient for c=∑h_i(1-h_{i+d}) was:
 d=+e: g[:n-e] +=wp(1-h[e:]); g[e:] -=wp(h[:-e])
 d=-e: g[e:] +=wn(1-h[:-e]); g[:n-e] -=wn(h[e:])
This is swapped. Which one matches NumPy? We need use actual. Let's verify with p at boundary and c index for n=2. h=[a,b]. Actual full c for np.correlate(h,1-h) from formula out index center+d: d=-1: c=∑ h_{i-1}(1-h_i) valid i=1: h0(1-h1). d=0:a(1-a)+b(1-b). d=1: i=0: h1(1-h0). So c array [a(1-b), c0, b(1-a)]. Derivative wrt a: d=-1: -b, d=0:1-2a, d=1:1-a. Our loop e=1: wp=w1 (d=+1), wn=w-1. wp: g[e:]=g[1:]+=wp(1-h0)=w1(1-a); g[:1]-=wp h1 => g0-=w1 b. wn: g[:1]+=wn(1-h1)=wn(1-b); g[1:]-=wn h0=-wn a. d0: w0(1-2a),w0(1-2b). Total g0=w-1(1-b)-w1 b+w0(1-2a). Actual g0: derivative c-1 a(1-b) wrt a =1-b (weight wn), c0 1-2a, c1 b(1-a) wrt a=-b (weight wp). Good. g1= w1(1-a)-w-1 a + w0(1-2b). Actual: c-1 wrt b=-a (wn), c1 wrt b=1-a (wp). Good. So new gradient loop correct.

Update `weighted_grad_c` accordingly.

```
def run(...):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-15
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 try base...
 n=len(base) if even else len-1
 h_best=project_sum(base[:n])
 best_c=compute_c5(h_best)
 # quick CD
 h,c = cd_polish(h_best,n,30,deadline-600? rng)
 if c<best_c: update
 # Full L-BFGS from best if time enough
 if time.time() < deadline-350:
    h1,c1 = lbfgs_full(h_best,n,[100,500,2000,10000], mu=5, maxiter=100, deadline=deadline-300)
    if c1<best_c: update
    # CD after full
    h2,c2=cd_polish(best_h,n,40,deadline-150,rng)
    update
 # Anti basin if time
 if n%2==0 and time.time()<deadline-200:
    a=anti_from_h(h_best)[:n//2]
    hA,cA,a = lbfgs_anti(a, [100,500,2000,10000], maxiter=120, deadline=deadline-100)
    if cA<best: update
    hB=anti_h(a); hB,cB=cd_polish(hB,n,60,deadline-80,rng); update
    # random anti restarts
    for r in range(3) if time:
       ar=random_anti_init(n//2, 8+5*r, rng)
       hAr,cAr,aR=lbfgs_anti(ar,[100,1000,5000],80,deadline-80)
       hBr,cBr=cd_polish(anti_h(aR),n,30,deadline-80,rng)
       update
 # Random full restarts
 for r in range(5) if time:
    sig=0.05*(r+1)
    hp=project_sum(h_best + rng.normal(0,sig,n),n)
    # maybe L-BFGS with one beta
    hL,cL=lbfgs_full(hp,n,[1000],mu=5,maxiter=60,deadline=deadline-60)
    hCD,cCD=cd_polish(hL,n,30,deadline-60,rng)
    update
 # Final CD long
 remaining = max(10, deadline-5)
 hF,cF=cd_polish(best_h,n,1000, remaining, rng)
 update
 # optional final small lambda CD
 if time< final-20: hlam,clam=cd_polish(best_h,n,20,final-5,lam=0.05); update
 # final project
 return project_sum(best_h), compute, n
```

Implement `lbfgs_full`:
```
def lbfgs_full(h, n, betas, mu, maxiter, deadline, use_noise=False, rng=None):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h)
    target=n/2.0
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    for beta in betas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
        x0=best_h.copy()
        if use_noise and rng is not None:
            x0=project_sum(x0 + rng.normal(0,0.02,n),n)
        try:
            res=minimize(_full_f, x0, args=(beta,mu,target), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=_full_g, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-6,'maxls':20})
            cand=np.clip(res.x,0,1)
        except Exception:
            cand=None
        if cand is not None:
            cand=project_sum(cand,n)
            c=compute_c5(cand)
            if c<best_c:
                best_c=c; best_h=cand
    return best_h,best_c
```
But `_full_g` recomputes objective, okay. Mu maybe 10. If sum error large, penalty. Use mu=10? The full CD projection handles sum; L-BFGS just suggests. Use mu=1.0 to not dominate. The variant with equality not critical. Maybe use `mu=1.0`.

```
def lbfgs_anti(a, betas, maxiter, deadline):
    a=np.clip(a,0,1)
    h=anti_h(a); best_c=compute_c5(h); best_a=a
    m=len(a); bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
    for beta in betas:
       if time.time()>deadline-30: break
       try: res=minimize(_anti_f,best_a,args=(beta,),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=_anti_g,options=...)
       except: continue
       cand=np.clip(res.x,0,1)
       h=anti_h(cand); c=compute_c5(h)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_a=cand
       # also maybe project? anti sum exact.
    return anti_h(best_a), best_c, best_a
```

Implement `choose_quad_interval`:
```
def choose_quad_interval(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2):
    if x1 > x2: x1,x2 = x2,x1
    c0 = None
    best_dist = 1e100
    if lo <= x1 + 1e-12:
        end = x1 if x1 < hi else hi
        if end >= lo - 1e-12:
            mid = 0.5*(lo+end)
            dist = abs(mid - h0)
            c0 = (lo, end); best_dist = dist
    if hi >= x2 - 1e-12:
        start = x2 if x2 > lo else lo
        if start <= hi + 1e-12:
            mid = 0.5*(start+hi)
            dist = abs(mid - h0)
            if c0 is None or dist < best_dist - 1e-12:
                c0 = (start, hi); best_dist = dist
    if c0 is None:
        return None
    lo1, hi1 = c0
    if lo1 < 0.0: lo1=0.0
    if hi1 > 1.0: hi1=1.0
    val = 0.5*(lo1+hi1)
    return float(val)
```
If segment length negative due tolerance? We checked. If x1 or x2 outside, okay.

`solve_coord_fast` as above. Need use `math` imported. When `D` negative small, set 0. If D huge? fine.

`cd_polish`:
```
def cd_polish(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng=None, lam=0.0):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = cur
    base = np.arange(n)
    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        if rng is not None:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        else:
            shift = (it * 97) % n
            order = np.concatenate((base[shift:], base[:shift]))
        changed = 0
        for p in order:
            if (p % 31 == 0) and time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h0 = h[p]
            if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-13:
                continue
            x, a = solve_coord_fast(c, h, p, n, lam)
            if a is None:
                continue
            delta = x - h0
            if abs(delta) <= 1e-13:
                continue
            R = p
            n1p = n - 1 - p
            if n1p > R: R = n1p
            m0 = n - 1 - R
            c[m0:m0 + 2*R + 1] += delta * a
            c[n - 1] -= delta * delta
            h[p] = x
            changed += 1
        if changed == 0:
            break
        err = h.sum() - 0.5 * n
        if abs(err) > 1e-9:
            h = project_sum(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        cur = float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))
        if cur < best_c:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c
```
If deadline mid inner break, after inner pass not complete; we still project? The pass may have partial updates; projection and eval okay. But if break due time, may not have changed? We'll still do projection and might exceed deadline; okay. In final, return best.

Need ensure `order` values are ints; `p % 31`. If time check inside, break only inner, then pass ends, may project; could slightly overrun but okay.

`project_sum`: Use target n/2. Need if n maybe int. Implement top-level:
```
def project_sum(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(np.array(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-13:
        return h
    lo = -1.0; hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        p = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps > target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    p = np.clip(h + 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    err = target - float(p.sum())
    if abs(err) > 1e-14:
        interior = (p > 1e-12) & (p < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        cnt = float(interior.sum())
        if cnt > 0:
            p[interior] = np.clip(p[interior] + err / cnt, 0.0, 1.0)
    return p
```
If after err correction clipping changes sum, could loop twice. Add second bisection? Simpler call bisection again if after correction sum off. But float okay. To ensure exact, do bisection on `p` again? Maybe after adding err to interior, might hit bounds if err large; but err small. Could do:
```
for _ in range(2): ...
```
Good.

`anti_h`:
```
def anti_h(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1)
    h=np.empty(2*a.size)
    h[:a.size]=a
    h[a.size:]=1.0-a
    return h
```

`anti_from_h`:
```
def anti_from_h(h):
    n=len(h); m=n//2
    a=0.5*(h[:m] + 1.0 - h[m:])
    return anti_h(a)
```
If n odd, truncates; parent likely even.

`random_anti_init`:
```
def random_anti_init(m, K, rng):
    t=np.arange(m, dtype=float)
    a=np.full(m,0.5)
    for k in range(1,K+1):
       amp=0.30/np.sqrt(float(k))
       phase=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi)
       a += amp * np.cos(2.0*np.pi*k*t/m + phase)
    return np.clip(a,0.0,1.0)
```
Amplitude maybe large; clip. K up to 15.

`random_full_init`:
```
def random_full_init(n, K, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    h=np.full(n,0.5)
    for k in range(1,K+1):
       amp=0.25/np.sqrt(float(k))
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       h += amp*np.cos(2π*k*x/2 + phase)
    return project_sum(h,n)
```
Maybe not used.

L-BFGS smooth functions:
`smooth_part(c, beta)` returns F_unscaled and weights? For full/anti, avoid duplicate.
```
def lse_weights(c,beta):
   maxc=float(np.max(c))
   sc=beta*(c-maxc)
   e=np.exp(sc)
   s=float(e.sum())
   w=e/s
   val=maxc + math.log(s)/beta
   return val, w
```
Since beta positive. If s underflows? max exp 1 so s>=1. Good.

`weighted_grad_c(h,w)`:
```
def weighted_grad_c(h,w):
   n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n)
   g += w[n-1]*(1.0-2.0*h)
   for e in range(1,n):
       wp = w[n-1+e]; wn = w[n-1-e]
       if wp != 0.0:
          g[e:] += wp*(1.0-h[:n-e])
          g[:n-e] -= wp*h[e:]
       if wn != 0.0:
          g[:n-e] += wn*(1.0-h[e:])
          g[e:] -= wn*h[:n-e]
   return g
```
Order: For e=1, `h[:n-e]` and `h[e:]`. Good. Note for wn d=-e: formula g[:n-e] += wn*(1-h[e:]); g[e:] -= wn*h[:n-e]. Good.

Could optimize if `wp+wn ==0` but both zero skip. Values may be tiny; use `if wp != 0.0`. Floating underflow yields exact 0. Good.

`_full_fg`:
```
def _full_fg(x, beta, mu, target):
    n=len(x)
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    val,w=lse_weights(c,beta)
    dx=2.0/n
    val *= dx
    g=weighted_grad_c(x,w) * dx
    s=float(x.sum())
    pen=0.5*mu*(s-target)*(s-target)
    val += pen
    if mu != 0.0:
       g += mu*(s-target)
    return val,g
```
`np.clip` each eval copies; okay. L-BFGS should give x in bounds, but safety.

`_full_f/g` separate:
```
def _full_f(x,beta,mu,target): return _full_fg(x,beta,mu,target)[0]
```

`_anti_fg`:
```
def _anti_fg(a,beta):
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1.0)
   m=len(a); h=np.empty(2*m); h[:m]=a; h[m:]=1.0-a
   c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
   val,w=lse_weights(c,beta)
   dx=1.0/m  # n=2m dx=2/(2m)=1/m
   val *= dx
   gh=weighted_grad_c(h,w)
   ga=(gh[:m] - gh[m:]) * dx
   return val, ga
```
No penalty.

We can make `lbfgs_full` short:
```
def lbfgs_full(h,n,betas,mu,its,dl):
 best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h); t=n/2; bd=[(0,1)]*n
 for b in betas:
  if time.time()>dl-20: break
  try: res=minimize(_full_f,best_h,args=(b,mu,t),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bd,jac=_full_g,options={'maxiter':its,'gtol':1e-6,'ftol':1e-12})
  except Exception: continue
  cand=project_sum(np.clip(res.x,0,1),n); c=compute_c5(cand)
  if c<best_c: best_h,best_c=cand,c
 return best_h,best_c
```

Inside lbfgs_anti uses dl. Then CD with dl.

Random:
```
for r in range(4):
 if time.time()+80 > deadline-40: break
 hp=project_sum(best_h + rng.normal(0,0.05*(r+1),n),n)
 hc,cc=cd_polish(hp,n,20, min(deadline-40, time.time()+60),rng)
 update
```

Final CD:
```
if time.time()+5 < deadline:
 hf,cf=cd_polish(best_h,n,100000, deadline-2, rng)
 update
```
`max_passes` very large but deadline. If time remaining 2, skip.

After final, project and compute.

Let's ensure `cd_polish` with `max_passes=100000` doesn't waste if no changes; breaks.

Potential bug: In `cd_polish`, if deadline passed mid inner, it breaks inner, then may still project and evaluate, possibly exceeding deadline but small. At top of next pass checks. Good.

Now, check if code includes `math` used in solve. Yes.

Let's write final with line breaks. Need stay under 300 lines; I'll aim ~270.

Double-check syntax:

- `zero_max = float(np.max(cb[zero_mask])) if np.any(zero_mask) else -1e300`; `cb[zero_mask]` length. okay.
- `m_end = m0 + L`; `total_lags = 2*n - 1`; if m_end < total_lags: max.

- In CD loop, `x, a = solve_coord_fast(...)`; if a is None skip. If x==h0, a may be None or not. We check.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))


def project_sum(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(h.sum()) - target) < 1e-13:
        return h
    lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        p = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        if float(p.sum()) > target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    p = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    err = target - float(p.sum())
    if abs(err) > 1e-14:
        inner = (p > 1e-12) & (p < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        cnt = float(inner.sum())
        if cnt > 0.0:
            p[inner] = np.clip(p[inner] + err / cnt, 0.0, 1.0)
    return p


def anti_h(a):
    a = np.clip(np.asarray(a, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    m = a.size
    h = np.empty(2 * m, dtype=float)
    h[:m] = a
    h[m:] = 1.0 - a
    return h


def anti_from_h(h):
    n = len(h)
    m = n // 2
    a = 0.5 * (h[:m] + 1.0 - h[m:])
    return anti_h(a)


def random_anti_init(m, kmax, rng):
    t = np.arange(m, dtype=float)
    a = np.full(m, 0.5, dtype=float)
    for k in range(1, kmax + 1):
        amp = 0.30 / math.sqrt(float(k))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k * t / m + ph)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)


def lse_weights(c, beta):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    z = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
    s = float(z.sum())
    w = z / s
    return cmax + math.log(s) / beta, w


def weighted_grad_c(h, w):
    n = len(h)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    g = w[n - 1] * (omh - h)
    for e in range(1, n):
        wp = w[n - 1 + e]
        wn = w[n - 1 - e]
        if wp != 0.0:
            g[e:] += wp * omh[:n - e]
            g[:n - e] -= wp * h[e:]
        if wn != 0.0:
            g[:n - e] += wn * omh[e:]
            g[e:] -= wn * h[:n - e]
    return g


def _full_fg(x, beta, mu, target):
    n = len(x)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    val, w = lse_weights(c, beta)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    val *= dx
    g = weighted_grad_c(x, w) * dx
    s = float(x.sum())
    if mu != 0.0:
        val += 0.5 * mu * (s - target) * (s - target)
        g += mu * (s - target)
    return val, g


def _full_f(x, beta, mu, target):
    return _full_fg(x, beta, mu, target)[0]


def _full_g(x, beta, mu, target):
    return _full_fg(x, beta, mu, target)[1]


def _anti_fg(a, beta):
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)
    m = a.size
    h = np.empty(2 * m, dtype=float)
    h[:m] = a
    h[m:] = 1.0 - a
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    val, w = lse_weights(c, beta)
    dx = 1.0 / m
    val *= dx
    gh = weighted_grad_c(h, w)
    ga = (gh[:m] - gh[m:]) * dx
    return val, ga


def _anti_f(a, beta):
    return _anti_fg(a, beta)[0]


def _anti_g(a, beta):
    return _anti_fg(a, beta)[1]


def choose_quad_interval(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2):
    if x1 > x2:
        x1, x2 = x2, x1
    best = None
    best_dist = 1e100
    if lo <= x1 + 1e-12:
        end = min(hi, x1)
        if end >= lo - 1e-12:
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + end)
            best = (lo, end)
            best_dist = abs(mid - h0)
    if hi >= x2 - 1e-12:
        start = max(lo, x2)
        if start <= hi + 1e-12:
            mid = 0.5 * (start + hi)
            dist = abs(mid - h0)
            if best is None or dist < best_dist - 1e-12:
                best = (start, hi)
                best_dist = dist
    if best is None:
        return None
    lo1, hi1 = best
    if lo1 < 0.0:
        lo1 = 0.0
    if hi1 > 1.0:
        hi1 = 1.0
    return float(0.5 * (lo1 + hi1))


def solve_coord_fast(c, h, p, n, lam=0.0, iters=25):
    h0 = float(h[p])
    if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-14:
        return h0, None
    R = p
    q = n - 1 - p
    if q > R:
        R = q
    L = 2 * R + 1
    m0 = n - 1 - R
    d = np.arange(-R, R + 1)

    a = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    q1 = p - d
    v1 = (q1 >= 0) & (q1 < n)
    if np.any(v1):
        a[v1] += 1.0 - h[q1[v1]]
    q2 = p + d
    v2 = (q2 >= 0) & (q2 < n)
    if np.any(v2):
        a[v2] -= h[q2[v2]]

    if lam != 0.0:
        av = a + lam
    else:
        av = a

    cb = c[m0:m0 + L]
    diag = R
    cd = float(cb[diag])
    ad = float(av[diag])

    unaff = -1e300
    if m0 > 0:
        unaff = float(np.max(c[:m0]))
    mend = m0 + L
    if mend < 2 * n - 1:
        if float(np.max(c[mend:])) > unaff:
            unaff = float(np.max(c[mend:]))

    cur_max = float(np.max(cb))
    if unaff > cur_max:
        cur_max = unaff

    pos_idx = np.where(av > 1e-14)[0]
    neg_idx = np.where(av < -1e-14)[0]
    zero_mask = ~(pos_idx > -1) & ~(neg_idx > -1)
    zero_mask = (av <= 1e-14) & (av >= -1e-14)
    zero_max = float(np.max(cb[zero_mask])) if np.any(zero_mask) else -1e300

    cb_pos = cb[pos_idx]
    a_pos = av[pos_idx]
    cb_neg = cb[neg_idx]
    a_neg = av[neg_idx]

    L_hi = cur_max
    L_lo = 0.0 if lam == 0.0 else -abs(lam) - 1.0
    if L_hi < L_lo:
        L_hi = L_lo

    for _ in range(iters):
        if L_hi - L_lo < 1e-13:
            break
        Lv = 0.5 * (L_lo + L_hi)
        lo, hi = 0.0, 1.0
        ok = True
        if zero_max > Lv + 1e-12:
            ok = False
        if ok:
            if pos_idx.size:
                ub = h0 + float(np.min((Lv - cb_pos) / a_pos))
                if ub < hi:
                    hi = ub
            if neg_idx.size:
                lb = h0 + float(np.max((Lv - cb_neg) / a_neg))
                if lb > lo:
                    lo = lb
            if unaff > -1e299:
                if lam > 1e-14:
                    ub = h0 + (Lv - unaff) / lam
                    if ub < hi:
                        hi = ub
                elif lam < -1e-14:
                    lb = h0 + (Lv - unaff) / lam
                    if lb > lo:
                        lo = lb
                elif unaff > Lv + 1e-12:
                    ok = False
        if ok and lo <= hi + 1e-12:
            disc = ad * ad + 4.0 * (cd - Lv)
            if disc >= -1e-12:
                if disc < 0.0:
                    disc = 0.0
                sq = math.sqrt(disc)
                x1 = h0 + 0.5 * (ad - sq)
                x2 = h0 + 0.5 * (ad + sq)
                if choose_quad_interval(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2) is None:
                    ok = False
        if ok:
            L_hi = Lv
        else:
            L_lo = Lv

    L = L_hi
    lo, hi = 0.0, 1.0
    if zero_max > L + 1e-11:
        return h0, None
    if pos_idx.size:
        hi = min(hi, h0 + float(np.min((L - cb_pos) / a_pos)))
    if neg_idx.size:
        lo = max(lo, h0 + float(np.max((L - cb_neg) / a_neg)))
    if unaff > -1e299:
        if lam > 1e-14:
            hi = min(hi, h0 + (L - unaff) / lam)
        elif lam < -1e-14:
            lo = max(lo, h0 + (L - unaff) / lam)
        elif unaff > L + 1e-11:
            return h0, None
    if lo > hi + 1e-9:
        return h0, None

    disc = ad * ad + 4.0 * (cd - L)
    if disc >= -1e-12:
        if disc < 0.0:
            disc = 0.0
        sq = math.sqrt(disc)
        x1 = h0 + 0.5 * (ad - sq)
        x2 = h0 + 0.5 * (ad + sq)
        x = choose_quad_interval(lo, hi, h0, x1, x2)
    else:
        x = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    if x is None:
        return h0, None
    x = float(np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0))
    if abs(x - h0) <= 1e-13:
        return h0, None
    return x, a


def cd_polish(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    cur = float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cur
    base = np.arange(n)

    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if rng is not None:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        else:
            sh = (it * 97) % n
            order = np.concatenate((base[sh:], base[:sh]))
        changed = 0
        for p in order:
            if (p % 31 == 0) and time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h0 = h[p]
            if h0 <= 1e-14 or h0 >= 1.0 - 1e-14:
                continue
            x, a = solve_coord_fast(c, h, p, n, 0.0, 25)
            if a is None:
                continue
            delta = x - h0
            if abs(delta) <= 1e-14:
                continue
            R = p
            q = n - 1 - p
            if q > R:
                R = q
            m0 = n - 1 - R
            c[m0:m0 + 2 * R + 1] += delta * a
            c[n - 1] -= delta * delta
            h[p] = x
            changed += 1
        if changed == 0:
            break
        if abs(float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * n) > 1e-9:
            h = project_sum(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        cur = float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))
        if cur < best_c:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def lbfgs_full(h, n, betas, mu, iters, dl):
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    bd = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for b in betas:
        if time.time() > dl - 25.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(_full_f, best_h, args=(b, mu, target),
                           method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bd, jac=_full_g,
                           options={"maxiter": iters, "ftol": 1e-12,
                                    "gtol": 1e-6, "maxls": 20})
            cand = project_sum(np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0), n)
        except Exception:
            continue
        cc = compute_c5(cand)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = cand
    return best_h, best_c


def lbfgs_anti(a, betas, iters, dl):
    best_a = np.clip(np.asarray(a, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    best_h = anti_h(best_a)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    m = best_a.size
    bd = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m
    for b in betas:
        if time.time() > dl - 25.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(_anti_f, best_a, args=(b,),
                           method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bd, jac=_anti_g,
                           options={"maxiter": iters, "ftol": 1e-12,
                                    "gtol": 1e-6, "maxls": 20})
            cand = np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0)
            hc = anti_h(cand)
        except Exception:
            continue
        cc = compute_c5(hc)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_a = cand
            best_h = hc
    return best_h, best_a, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = max(t0 + 1.0, t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if arr.size == 0:
            raise ValueError
        n = len(arr)
        if n % 2:
            n -= 1
        if n < 100:
            n = 700
            h_best = anti_h(random_anti_init(n // 2, 8, rng))
        else:
            h_best = project_sum(arr[:n].copy(), n)
    except Exception:
        n = 700
        h_best = anti_h(random_anti_init(n // 2, 8, rng))

    h_best = project_sum(h_best, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(h_best)

    d1 = min(deadline, t0 + max(30.0, 0.25 * float(budget_s)))
    if time.time() < d1:
        h1, c1 = cd_polish(h_best, n, 120, d1, rng)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            h_best = h1

    if time.time() + 170.0 < deadline - 80.0:
        dl = time.time() + 170.0
        h2, c2 = lbfgs_full(h_best, n, (200.0, 1000.0, 5000.0), 1.0, 45, dl)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            h_best = h2
        if time.time() + 70.0 < deadline - 40.0:
            h3, c3 = cd_polish(h_best, n, 60, min(deadline - 40.0, time.time() + 70.0), rng)
            if c3 < best_c:
                best_c = c3
                h_best = h3

    if (n % 2 == 0) and time.time() + 230.0 < deadline - 80.0:
        m = n // 2
        starts = [anti_from_h(h_best)[:m],
                  random_anti_init(m, 7, rng),
                  random_anti_init(m, 15, rng)]
        for a0 in starts:
            if time.time() + 90.0 > deadline - 60.0:
                break
            dl = min(deadline - 60.0, time.time() + 65.0)
            ah, aa, ac = lbfgs_anti(a0, (80.0, 700.0, 3500.0), 70, dl)
            if ac < best_c:
                best_c = ac
                h_best = ah
            if time.time() + 50.0 < deadline - 30.0:
                h4, c4 = cd_polish(anti_h(aa), n, 50, min(deadline - 30.0, time.time() + 50.0), rng)
                if c4 < best_c:
                    best_c = c4
                    h_best = h4

    for r in range(4):
        if time.time() + 55.0 > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        sig = 0.03 + 0.03 * r
        hp = project_sum(h_best + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n), n)
        h5, c5 = cd_polish(hp, n, 25, min(deadline - 30.0, time.time() + 50.0), rng)
        if c5 < best_c:
            best_c = c5
            h_best = h5

    if time.time() + 5.0 < deadline:
        hf, cf = cd_polish(h_best, n, 1000000, deadline - 2.0, rng)
        if cf < best_c:
            best_c = cf
            h_best = hf

    h_best = project_sum(h_best, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(h_best)
    return h_best, best_c, n
```
